# Jordan — English books

**JORDAN OPENED AND COMPLETED 2026-11-03 under the user's
all-closed-countries ruling.** Jordan is CLOSED in the library.
Its English set as recorded is 64 files — Grades 1–8 and
10–12 (there are **no Grade 9 English entries in the record**;
the G9 folder holds other subjects). Two series carry the
whole set, both published for Jordan's National Center for
Curriculum Development (NCCD, later "…and Evaluation"),
Amman, from Pearson Education Ltd / York Press Ltd
originals: **Jordan Team Together** for Grades 1–7 and
**Jordan High Note** for Grades 8, 10, 11 and 12. Each grade
runs two semesters, a pupil/student book + activity/workbook
+ teacher's book per semester where the record holds them.
Printed page = PDF page − 2 in every one of the 64 books.

**Grab state 2026-11-03:** no reading copies were on disk
(the staging folders hold the record, not the files), so all
64 were pulled from the secured GitHub library through the
pusher's credential route — 57 from the `mid-size-books-2`
and `mid-size-books-5` releases, 6 from the repo tree, 1
from the staging copy still on disk. Every copy with a
recorded size matches it byte-for-byte, and every book's
identity was verified from its rendered cover/imprint —
which produced the corrections below.

**Read state 2026-11-03: all 64 files FULLY READ +
CITATION-CHECKED and filed in full below** (anchors per
book 16–21, all passed; 18/18 is the mode).

**Record corrections found by the covers (library-side
fixes owed):**
1. **The G1 "student term-1" file is the Semester 2 Pupil's
   Book** (Team Together, ISBN 978-9923-41-727-0). Grade 1's
   pupil books are complete; there is no older-ministry-series
   G1 book in this set at all — all nine G1 files are Team
   Together, in two editions (1st ed. 2024 and 2nd ed. rev.
   2025, both filed).
2. **The G7 "term-2" student and workbook files are Semester
   1 books** (ISBN trio …-41-917-5 / …-918-2 / …-919-9 with
   the Term 1 teacher book, which keys the pupil book
   page-for-page). Grade 7 in hand is a complete Semester 1
   trio; **no Grade 7 Semester 2 book of any kind is in the
   record — those are still to find.**
3. **The two G1 Teacher Semester 2 files are the same book
   in two digital copies** (same ISBN, imprint and text) —
   a duplicate in the library.
4. **The paired Term 1 workbooks at G11 and G12 are edition
   pairs, not different books:** G11 = 1st ed. 2024 (72 pp,
   file 9667) + 2nd ed. rev. 2025 (52 pp); G12 = 1st ed.
   (trial) 2025 (file 30953) + 2nd ed. 2026 (adopted
   2026/2027 — newer than the rest of its set). The G11
   2024 teacher book keys workbook lessons that exist only
   in the 72-page edition.
5. **Grade 8 holds Semester 1 only** (Student's Book +
   Workbook, High Note) — no Semester 2 books and no
   teacher books, so no audioscripts or B-lesson keys exist
   in the held G8 set.
6. **Edition seams run through the set:** pupil/activity
   books are repeatedly a newer edition than the teacher
   book filed beside them (G2, G3 Sem 1, G4 Sem 1, G6).
   Several Semester 2 sets are watermarked proof/trial
   copies ("copy under preparation and review") — complete
   and legible, but proof-state PDFs.
7. **The G3 Teacher's Book Semester 1 teaches a festival
   unit (Calgary Stampede) that the Jordan Pupil's Book
   dropped** — the guide was never updated to match.

**Series-final verdicts (from the G12 filing):** essay
writing is fully taught by G12 (for-and-against, general
and opinion essays with models and graphic organisers);
conditionals are fully taught including inverted forms;
the passive is taught as a tense system — but the causative
(*have/get something done*) appears nowhere in Grade 12,
and reported speech arrives only in advanced dress
(reporting verbs + impersonal structures), with no basic
backshift unit at the top of the course.

# Jordan — English Grade 1 Jordan Team Together, Grade 1: Pupil's Book, Semester 2 — FULLY READ + CITATION CHECK PASSED 2026-11-03 (anchors 18/18)

> ⚠️ IDENTITY CORRECTION: The source file is named `grade-01_gov_jordan_english-language_student_term-1.pdf` and the project records list it as the Grade 1 Term 1 student book from minhaji.net (download 10213). The book inside is NOT a Term 1 book and NOT an older ministry series. Its cover, imprint and contents all identify it as the **Semester 2** Pupil's Book. This file is therefore the Grade 1 Term 2 / Semester 2 pupil book whose absence was previously recorded — the set DOES contain it, under a wrong filename. There is no separate older-series Grade 1 pupil book in this set at all.

## The book itself
- Verified title (cover + National Library indexing box, PDF p.2): *Jordan Team Together, Grade 1: Pupil's Book, Semester 2*.
- Series: Jordan Team Together (Jordan adaptation of Pearson's Team Together), published for Jordan's National Center for Curriculum Development (NCCD), Hashemite Kingdom of Jordan, Ministry of Education.
- Author (cover/contents page and rights page): Anna Osborn, with Stephen Thompson. Thanks on the rights page to Stephen Thompson, Sian Mavor and Wendy Cherry. © Pearson Education Limited and York Press Ltd. 2024 (Pearson + York Press logos on the contents page).
- Edition: First edition (trial), 2024 / 1445 AH. ISBN 978-9923-41-727-0. National Library deposit number (2024/10/5988). Adopted for Jordanian schools by Supreme Council decision No. 9/2024 (meeting No. 6/2024, 17/11/2024) for the 2024/2025 academic year.
- Cover committee: "Evaluation and Adaptation Committee" — Prof. Nayel Darweesh Al-Shara'h (Head), Dr. Manal Fahed Abuarman (Coordinator), Prof. Abdallah Ahmad Bani Abdelrahman, Prof. Sabri Shehadeh AlShboul, Dr. Zeina Mohammad AlKaraki, Dr. Adnan Ahmad Al-Omari.
- Copy facts: source file 34,731,388 bytes; 56 PDF pages (pdfinfo). Printed pagination = PDF page − 2 throughout the body (PDF p.6 = printed p.4; cutout sheets at the back carry printed folios in the 40s–50s). Front matter paginated i–ii in roman numerals.
- Text layer: present on all pages (sparse on illustrated pupil pages, as expected; some letter glyphs extract imperfectly, e.g. initial letters of "frog/giraffe" on the Unit 7 picture-dictionary page). No image-only page ranges; identity and contents verified from rendered cover/imprint/contents pages as well as the text layer.
- Structure shared with Semester 1: every unit has 11 lessons — Lessons 1–2 Vocabulary, Lessons 3–4 and 7–8 Language practice, Lessons 5–6 Story, Lessons 9–10 Phonics, Lesson 11 Project — plus a Picture Dictionary page per unit, then Meg's Learning Club and back-matter Cutouts (project sheets for Units 5–8).

## Full unit/lesson map
Printed page numbers; PDF page = printed + 2.
- **Welcome — Who's this? (printed p.4; PDF pp.6–7).** Recap unit: characters re-introduce themselves by name (own-name and "This is my …" frames), classroom objects (bag, book, crayon, eraser, pen, pencil, pencil case, ruler) and toys (ball, car, doll, plane, robot, teddy, train, yoyo) recalled; numbers and colours recycled on the second spread.
- **Unit 5 — My body (printed pp.6–13; PDF pp.8–15).** Vocabulary: arms, body, face, feet, hair, hands, head, legs. Grammar: has got for description — "He's got … / She's got …", negatives "He hasn't got … / She hasn't got …". Story (Lessons 5–6): listen-and-follow with a point-to-the-train task. Phonics: Hh/Ll (head, hair, hand, hat; leaf, legs, lion, log), then Gg/Ff (gate, girl, goat, green; face, feet, five, four). Project (Lesson 11): A funny friend (model figure). Picture Dictionary: Body.
- **Unit 6 — My blue jacket (printed pp.14–21; PDF pp.16–23).** Vocabulary (clothes): boots, dress, jacket, jeans, jumper, skirt, socks, T-shirt. Grammar: "How are you? — I'm fine, thank you"; "Where's my …? — It's on the …"; "I'm wearing …". Story task: point to the blue jacket. Phonics: Jj/Uu (jacket, jam, jeans, jelly, jump; umbrella, under, up), then Oo/Nn (octopus, olive, on, orange; nest, net, nine, nut). Project: Paper dolls. Picture Dictionary: Clothes.
- **Unit 7 — They're tigers! (printed pp.22–29; PDF pp.24–31).** Vocabulary (animals): frog, giraffe, lizard, monkey, snake, spider, tiger, zebra. Grammar: "What are these? — They're …" (plural identification). Story task: point to May. Phonics: Zz/Kk/Qq (zebra, zero, zoo; king, kite, kitten; queen, question, quiet), then Xx/Vv/Yy (box, fox, six; van, vet, violin; yellow, yoghurt, yoyo) — completion of the alphabet begun in Semester 1. Project: Animal masks. Picture Dictionary: Animals.
- **Unit 8 — I like cakes (printed pp.30–37; PDF pp.32–39).** Vocabulary (food): apples, cakes, carrots, grapes, juice, lemonade, lemons, sweets. Grammar: "I like … / I don't like …"; polite requests "Yes, please." / "Thank you." Story task: point to Meg and Tom. Phonics: CVC word building and blending (e.g. log, vet, bus, hat, hen, pan, bag, fox, jug, pot, sun, yes). Project: A food plate. Picture Dictionary: Food.
- **Learning Club (printed pp.38–40; PDF pp.40–41).** Meg's Learning Club with Language booster: road-safety language (stop / cross here / walk signs; see the matching Teacher's Book notes) plus listening and circling tasks (audio LC1.1–LC1.4).
- **Back matter (PDF pp.42–56).** Image credits (PDF p.42), then Cutouts: Unit 5 Project sheets ×2, Unit 6 Project sheets ×2, Unit 7/Unit 8 Project sheets ×2, Unit 8 Project sheet (printed folios continue to the low 50s); final PDF pages are blank versos.

## Grammar and vocabulary spine for the book
- Vocabulary sets: body (8), clothes (8), animals (8), food (8), recycled classroom objects and toys in the Welcome unit.
- Grammar spine: has got / hasn't got for physical description (Unit 5) → location of clothing "Where's my …? It's on the …" and present-continuous-style "I'm wearing …" (Unit 6) → plural demonstrative question "What are these? They're …" (Unit 7) → likes/dislikes "I like / I don't like …" with polite request formulas (Unit 8). This continues Semester 1's spine (be, have got, prepositions of place) without repeating its teaching.
- Phonics spine: completes the single-letter alphabet — h, l, g, f (Unit 5); j, u, o, n (Unit 6); z, k, q, x, v, y (Unit 7) — then consolidates into CVC blending (Unit 8). With Semester 1's s, d, c, a, p, r, e, b, t, m, w, i, the two semesters cover all 26 letters.
- Skills pattern per scope-and-sequence: listening = recognising familiar nouns/adjectives and main information in slow, simple speech; speaking = single-word repetition, basic phrases, simple songs with picture support; reading = following simple dialogues in short illustrated stories; writing = tracing individual letters and writing familiar words.

## Honest negatives
- The book does NOT teach: numbers beyond the Semester 1 set (no new number work beyond recycling), days/months/seasons (days of the week sit in Semester 1's Learning Club), family or school vocabulary as new material (recycled only), any past or future tense, articles/plurals as explicit grammar beyond "They're …", or free writing beyond tracing and familiar-word writing.
- No answer key, audioscripts or tests are printed in the pupil book (they live in the Teacher's Book / digital resources); the audio itself is not in the PDF — every lesson references numbered audio tracks (e.g. 5.1–8.22, PD tracks, LC tracks) that require the separate digital resources.
- Copy defects: the final cutout section includes blank versos (PDF pp.44, 46, 48, 50, 52, 54–56 are blank or near-blank), which is faithful to the print sheets, not missing content. Text-layer extraction garbles some decorative lettering (unit headers extract as e.g. "Lessonn1 7"); the printed pages themselves are clean.
- Filing note: because this file was catalogued as "Term 1", any earlier Grade 1 record that treats the Semester 2 pupil book as missing is wrong; conversely the set holds NO Grade 1 pupil book from any older (pre–Team Together) Jordanian series.

# Jordan — English Grade 1 Jordan Team Together, Grade 1: Pupil's Book, Semester 1 — FULLY READ + CITATION CHECK PASSED 2026-11-03 (anchors 18/18)

## The book itself
- Verified title (cover + National Library indexing box, PDF p.2): *Jordan Team Together, Grade 1: Pupil's Book, Semester 1*. Filename `grade-01_gov_jordan_english-language_student_term-1-nccd.pdf` is accurate.
- Series: Jordan Team Together (Jordan adaptation of Pearson's Team Together), published for Jordan's National Center for Curriculum Development (NCCD), Ministry of Education, Hashemite Kingdom of Jordan.
- Author: Anna Osborn, with Stephen Thompson (contents page); rights page asserts Anna Osborn's authorship, with thanks to Stephen Thompson, Sian Mavor and Wendy Cherry. © Pearson Education Limited and York Press Ltd. — first edition (trial) 2024; this copy is the **Second edition (revised and updated), © 2025**.
- ISBN 978-9923-41-908-3. National Library deposit number (2025/3/1579). Adopted by Higher Council of the NCCD meeting No. 3/2024 (7/5/2024) and Board of Education decision No. 38/2024 (meeting No. 3/2024, 26/6/2024) for the 2025/2026 academic year. Printed 2024, reprinted 2025.
- Cover committee: "Adaptation Committee in Jordan" — Prof. Nayel Darweesh Al-Shara'h (Head), Dr. Manal Fahed Abuarman (Coordinator), Prof. Dina AbdulHameed Al-Jamal, Dr. Hamzah Ali Omari, Dr. Zeina Mohammad AlKaraki. (Note: a different, shorter committee list than the Semester 2 books' "Evaluation and Adaptation Committee".)
- Copy facts: source file 54,112,359 bytes; 56 PDF pages. Printed pagination = PDF page − 2 (PDF p.6 = printed p.4 "four"; cutouts carry printed folios 41–51). Front matter i–ii.
- Text layer: present throughout; illustrated pages extract sparsely and decorative headers garble (e.g. "Lessonn1 7"), but all vocabulary lists, scope-and-sequence tables and exercise instructions are legible. Identity verified from rendered cover/imprint/contents.
- Unit architecture (all four units): Lessons 1–2 Vocabulary, 3–4 Language practice, 5–6 Story Values, 7–8 Language practice, 9–10 Phonics, 11 Project, then a Picture Dictionary page.

## Full unit/lesson map
Printed page numbers; PDF page = printed + 2.
- **Welcome — Hello! (printed pp.4–5; PDF pp.6–7).** Characters introduced (Hala, Lama, Meg, Malek, Bill, Tom per the Teacher's Book); greetings "Hello! / Hi! / Good morning!"; numbers one–ten and colours blue, green, orange, red, yellow, brown.
- **Unit 1 — Family and friends (printed pp.6–13; PDF pp.8–15).** Vocabulary: boy, brother, cat, dad, friend, girl, mum, sister (pet: cat). Grammar: "What's your name? — My name's … / I'm …"; "How old are you? — I'm …"; "Who's this? — This is (my) …". Story (Lessons 5–6): "Find Ann", values strand. Phonics: Ss/Dd (sing, sister, six, sun; dad, dig, doll, duck), then Cc/Aa (cap, cat, cup, cut; Ann, ant, apple, arrow). Project: Finger puppets. Picture Dictionary: Family and friends (listen and trace).
- **Unit 2 — My school bag (printed pp.14–21; PDF pp.16–23).** Vocabulary: bag, book, crayon, eraser, pen, pencil, pencil case, ruler. Grammar: "I've got … / I haven't got …" for possession. Story: "Find Lama". Phonics: Pp/Rr (pen, pencil, pencil case, pink; rabbit, red, ruler, run), then Ee/Bb (egg, elbow, elephant; bag, ball, book, boy). Project: A school bag. Picture Dictionary: Classroom objects.
- **Unit 3 — Our classroom (printed pp.22–29; PDF pp.24–31).** Vocabulary: chair, desk, door, floor, teacher, wall, whiteboard, window. Grammar: "What's this? — It's a …"; place: "The book is on the chair. / The pens are on the floor." Story: "Find the pens". Phonics: Tt/Mm (teacher, teddy, ten, two; milk, moon, mouse, mum), then Ww/Ii (wall, water, wave, whiteboard; igloo, in, ink, insect). Project: A pen pot. Picture Dictionary: In the classroom.
- **Unit 4 — My favourite toy (printed pp.30–37; PDF pp.32–39).** Vocabulary: ball, car, doll, plane, robot, teddy, train, yoyo; feelings happy, sad. Grammar: "What colour is it? — It's …"; "I'm happy / sad"; "What's your favourite toy? — It's my …". Story: "Find Sue". Phonics: CVC words (bat, bed, cap, cat, map, mat, red, sad, sit, tap, web, wet) — reading and ordering CVC items. Project: A toy box. Picture Dictionary: Toys.
- **Learning Club (printed pp.38–40; PDF pp.40–41).** Meg's Learning Club + Language booster: days of the week (Sunday–Saturday), listening/circling day names (audio LC1.1–LC1.3 in this book; the Activity Book continues to LC1.5).
- **Back matter (PDF pp.42–56).** Image credits (PDF p.42), then Cutouts: project sheets for Units 1–4 (printed folios 41, 43, 45, 47, 49, 51) with blank versos; last pages blank.

## Grammar and vocabulary spine for the book
- Vocabulary: numbers 1–10 and 6 colours (Welcome); family + cat (8); school objects (8); classroom furniture/features (8); toys (8) + happy/sad.
- Grammar spine: self-introduction frames (name/age) and "Who's this? This is …" (Unit 1) → have got possession (Unit 2) → identification "What's this? It's a …" + on-prepositions for singular and plural subjects (Unit 3) → colour questions, feelings with be, and favourite-things frame (Unit 4).
- Phonics spine: 16 single letters in four pairs per unit — s/d, c/a; p/r, e/b; t/m, w/i — finishing with CVC blending in Unit 4. The remaining 10 letters are Semester 2 material.
- Writing throughout is tracing individual letters and, by Unit 4, writing familiar CVC words; reading is following simple dialogues in short illustrated stories.

## Honest negatives
- The book does NOT teach: the full alphabet (10 letters deferred to Semester 2), plural grammar beyond the fixed "The pens are on the floor" example, likes/dislikes, body/clothes/animals/food vocabulary (all Semester 2), telling the time, days of the week as core unit content (Learning Club extra only), or any sentence writing beyond tracing/familiar words.
- No answers, audioscripts or assessment material in the pupil book; all listening depends on external audio/digital resources referenced by track numbers (1.1–4.22, PD.1–PD.4, LC tracks).
- Copy defects: none affecting content; extraction garbles decorative headers only. The second-edition revision (this copy) post-dates the first-edition Teacher's Book also held in this set (see g01-teacher-term-1.md) — the pupil content the older guide describes includes Review lessons and Progress path pages that this second-edition pupil book does not contain in the same form.

# Jordan — English Grade 1 Jordan Team Together, Grade 1: Activity Book, Semester 1 — FULLY READ + CITATION CHECK PASSED 2026-11-03 (anchors 18/18)

> Copy note: This file (`..._student_term-1-workbook.pdf`, ex-minhaji) and the NCCD file (`..._student_term-1-workbook-nccd.pdf`, see g01-workbook-term-1-nccd.md) are the SAME book — same ISBN, same second edition, same contents and pagination. This copy differs only in distribution dressing: it carries the Minhaji platform logo on the imprint page, is stamped "Reprinted 2025–2026" (NCCD copy: "Reprinted 2025"), and its scan runs to 44 PDF pages because it includes the two sticker sheets at the back (PDF pp.41 and 43) that the NCCD print PDF omits. It is not a different or older workbook.

## The book itself
- Verified title (cover + indexing box, PDF p.2): *Jordan Team Together, Grade 1: Activity Book, Semester 1*.
- Series/publisher: Jordan Team Together; National Center for Curriculum Development **and Evaluation** (publisher line as printed on this copy's cover), Ministry of Education, Jordan. Author: Anna Osborn. © Pearson Education Limited and York Press Ltd. — first edition (trial) 2024; **Second edition (revised and updated) 2025**.
- ISBN 978-9923-41-909-0. National Library deposit (2025/3/1582). Adopted by Higher Council meeting No. 3/2024 (7/5/2024), Board of Education decision No. 39/2024 (26/6/2024) for 2025/2026. Printed 2024; this copy reprinted 2025–2026.
- Cover committee: "Adaptation Committee in Jordan" (Al-Shara'h head; Abuarman coordinator; Al-Jamal; Omari; AlKaraki) — same as the Semester 1 Pupil's Book.
- Copy facts: source file 11,993,841 bytes; 44 PDF pages. Printed pagination = PDF page − 2 (PDF p.4 = printed p.2). Body content ends at PDF p.40 (image credits); PDF pp.41–44 are sticker sheets and blanks.
- Text layer: present; exercise pages extract well. Identity verified from rendered cover/imprint/contents.
- Activity Book lesson pattern per unit mirrors the Pupil's Book but only even-numbered lessons are worked here: Lesson 2 (vocabulary), Lesson 4 (language practice), Lesson 6 (Story Values), Lesson 8 (language practice), Lessons 9–10 (phonics), followed after the four units by Learning Club, Vocabulary time, Fun time and Letters and sounds practice sections.

## Full unit/lesson map
Printed page numbers; PDF page = printed + 2.
- **Welcome — Hello! (printed pp.2–3; PDF pp.4–5).** Look, trace and draw (numbers); Colours and numbers: look and colour (blue, green, brown, orange, red, yellow).
- **Unit 1 — Family and friends (printed pp.4–9; PDF pp.6–11).** L2: listen, look and match; listen, find and stick (audio 1.3–1.4). L4 Language practice: listen and circle (1.8). L6 Story Values: listen, look and number (1.10); look and colour — Values: "Be helpful". L8: listen, find and stick (1.17). L9 Phonics Ss/Dd: follow, trace and say. L10 Phonics Cc/Aa: trace and say.
- **Unit 2 — My school bag (printed pp.10–15; PDF pp.12–17).** L2: listen, look and tick (2.3); listen, find and stick (2.4). L4: listen and match (2.8). L6 Story Values: listen, look and number (2.10); look and colour. L8: listen, find and stick (2.17). L9 Phonics Pp/Rr: trace and say; look, trace and say; match. L10 Phonics Ee/Bb: trace and say; match.
- **Unit 3 — Our classroom (printed pp.16–21; PDF pp.18–23).** L2: listen, look and number (3.3); listen and stick (3.4); trace. L4: listen and circle (3.8). L6 Story Values: listen, look and number (3.10); look and colour the circles. L8: listen and stick (3.17); trace. L9 Phonics Tt/Mm: trace, match and write; look, say and write (completing ten / mouse / teacher / mum patterns). L10 Phonics Ww/Ii: trace and say.
- **Unit 4 — My favourite toy (printed pp.22–27; PDF pp.24–29).** L2: listen, look and circle (4.3); listen and stick (4.4); trace. L4: listen and circle (4.8). L6 Story Values: listen, look and number (4.10); look and colour. L8: listen and stick (4.17); trace. L9–10 Phonics: CVC work headed "e-a-i" in this edition — listen, write and say (4.21); match (cat etc.); choose, write and say (map/cap-type items).
- **Learning Club (printed pp.28–29; PDF pp.30–31).** Days of the week: find-and-circle wordsearch (Sunday…Saturday); listen and match (LC1.5).
- **Vocabulary time (printed pp.30–33; PDF pp.32–35).** Four review spreads, one per unit: (1) look, trace and say — family set; (2) look, number and say — the 8 school objects in fixed order (bag, book, crayon, pen, pencil, pencil case, eraser, ruler); (3) look, match and say — classroom set; (4) look, tick and say — toys set.
- **Fun time (printed pp.34–35; PDF pp.36–37).** Fun time 1: count, write and trace (cats, books, windows, balls, T-shirts, trains). Fun time 2: look, colour and trace.
- **Letters and sounds practice (printed pp.36–37; PDF pp.38–39).** Trace and copy for S/s, D/d, C/c, A/a, P/p, R/r (spread 1) and E/e, B/b, T/t, M/m, W/w, I/i (spread 2).
- **Stickers (unfoliated; PDF pp.41, 43).** Sticker sheets keyed "Unit 1 Page 4 / Page 7", "Unit 2 Page 10 …", "Unit 3 Page 16 / Page 19", "Unit 4 Page 22 …" — the sheets the "find and stick" exercises use. PDF pp.42, 44 blank; PDF p.40 (printed sequence end) is image credits.

## Grammar and vocabulary spine for the book
- Pure consolidation of the Semester 1 Pupil's Book: same four vocabulary sets (family, school bag, classroom, toys), same grammar frames practised receptively (name/age introductions, have got, What's this/It's a, colour and favourite-toy questions) through listening-circle/tick/match/stick tasks rather than production.
- Phonics practice mirrors the pupil sequence s/d, c/a, p/r, e/b, t/m, w/i, then CVC; the Letters and sounds practice section is handwriting consolidation for the same 12 letters (CVC letters a/e/i appear inside word work).
- Workbook-only additions: Vocabulary time review spreads, two Fun time counting/colouring pages, and the days-of-the-week Learning Club wordsearch.

## Honest negatives
- The Activity Book teaches nothing new grammatically; it does NOT add writing composition, reading passages, or assessment tests — all tasks are listen/look/trace/colour/stick/match at single-word level.
- Odd-numbered Pupil's Book lessons (1, 3, 5, 7, 11) have no Activity Book pages; the Project lessons have no workbook support.
- Many exercises are unusable as designed without the printed sticker sheets and the audio (tracks 1.3–4.21, LC1.5); the PDF includes the sticker sheets only in this minhaji copy.
- Copy defects: none in content; the extra 4 PDF pages versus the NCCD copy are stickers/blanks, not extra teaching pages.

# Jordan — English Grade 1 Jordan Team Together, Grade 1: Activity Book, Semester 1 — FULLY READ + CITATION CHECK PASSED 2026-11-03 (anchors 18/18)

## Full unit/lesson map
Printed page numbers; PDF page = printed + 2. (Identical to the minhaji copy page-for-page.)
- **Welcome — Hello! (printed pp.2–3; PDF pp.4–5).** Look, trace and draw; Colours and numbers — look and colour.
- **Unit 1 — Family and friends (printed pp.4–9; PDF pp.6–11).** L2 listen/look/match and find-and-stick (1.3–1.4); L4 listen and circle (1.8); L6 Story Values — listen, look and number (1.10), Values: "Be helpful"; L8 listen, find and stick (1.17); L9 Phonics Ss/Dd follow, trace and say; L10 Phonics Cc/Aa trace and say.
- **Unit 2 — My school bag (printed pp.10–15; PDF pp.12–17).** L2 listen, look and tick (2.3), find and stick (2.4); L4 listen and match (2.8); L6 Story Values (2.10); L8 (2.17); L9 Phonics Pp/Rr trace and say + match; L10 Phonics Ee/Bb trace and say + match.
- **Unit 3 — Our classroom (printed pp.16–21; PDF pp.18–23).** L2 listen, look and number (3.3), listen and stick (3.4), trace; L4 listen and circle (3.8); L6 Story Values (3.10); L8 listen and stick (3.17) + trace; L9 Phonics Tt/Mm trace, match and write + look, say and write; L10 Phonics Ww/Ii trace and say.
- **Unit 4 — My favourite toy (printed pp.22–27; PDF pp.24–29).** L2 listen, look and circle (4.3), stick (4.4), trace; L4 listen and circle (4.8); L6 Story Values (4.10); L8 (4.17); L9 Phonics CVC — listen, write and say (4.21), match; L10 choose, write and say.
- **Learning Club (printed pp.28–29; PDF pp.30–31).** Days-of-week wordsearch (find and circle); listen and match (LC1.5).
- **Vocabulary time (printed pp.30–33; PDF pp.32–35).** One review spread per unit: family (look, trace and say), school objects (look, number and say — bag, book, crayon, pen, pencil, pencil case, eraser, ruler), classroom (look, match and say), toys (look, tick and say).
- **Fun time (printed pp.34–35; PDF pp.36–37).** Fun time 1: count, write and trace (cats, books, windows, balls, T-shirts, trains; folio "thirty-four 34"). Fun time 2: look, colour and trace ("thirty-five 35").
- **Letters and sounds practice (printed pp.36–37; PDF pp.38–39).** Trace and copy: S/s, D/d, C/c, A/a, P/p, R/r; then E/e, B/b, T/t, M/m, W/w, I/i.
- **Image credits (PDF p.40).**

## Grammar and vocabulary spine for the book
- Identical spine to the minhaji copy: receptive consolidation of the Pupil's Book Semester 1 sets and frames (introductions, have got, What's this?, colour/favourites), phonics s–i plus CVC, handwriting practice, days of the week in the Learning Club.

## Honest negatives
- No new grammar or vocabulary beyond the Pupil's Book; no tests, no reading texts, no free writing.
- This NCCD copy is functionally incomplete for classroom use as designed: the "find and stick" exercises (e.g. 1.4, 1.17, 2.4, 2.17, 3.4, 4.4) require sticker sheets that are not in this PDF (they survive only in the minhaji copy's back pages).
- Audio-dependent throughout (tracks cited per exercise); no audioscripts or answers printed in the Activity Book itself.
- Copy defects: none in the 40 pages present; the file simply ends after image credits.

> Edition note: This is the FIRST-edition (2024) Teacher's Book for Semester 1 (minhaji copy). ## The book itself
- Verified title (cover + indexing box, PDF p.2): *Jordan Team Together, Grade 1: Teacher's Book, Semester 1*.
- Series/publisher: Jordan Team Together; National Center for Curriculum Development, Ministry of Education, Jordan. Teacher's Book author (rights page): **Nick Coates**; thanks to Sian Mavor and Wendy Cherry. © Pearson Education Limited and York Press Ltd. 2024.
- Edition: **First edition (trial), 2024 / 1445**. ISBN 978-9923-41-605-1. National Library deposit (2024/4/2390). Adopted by Supreme Council of the National Curriculum Center meeting No. 3/2024 (7/5/2024), Board of Education decision No. 40/2024 (26/6/2024) for 2024/2025.
- Cover committee: "Evaluation and Adaptation Committee" (Al-Shara'h head; Al-Jamal; Omari; AlKaraki) as printed on this copy.
- Copy facts: source file 34,632,050 bytes; 112 PDF pages. Printed = PDF − 2 (Notes pages carry printed folios 105–109 at PDF pp.107–111; image credits close the book at PDF p.112).
- Text layer: full and rich (the guide reproduces pupil/activity pages in facsimile plus notes). Identity verified from rendered cover/imprint.
- What a Teacher's Book adds, in this series: scope-and-sequence, introduction and course-component guide, unit walkthrough, assessment guidance, "How to…" method pages (phonics, projects), a Games bank, a Classroom language bank, then per-lesson teaching notes for every Pupil's Book and Activity Book lesson with presentation/practice steps, extra activities (TPR, fast finishers, extension), answer keys embedded in the notes, a Glossary with phonetic transcriptions, and Notes pages. No separate audioscripts or test booklet is bound in.

## Full unit/lesson map
PDF page numbers (printed = PDF − 2).
- **Front matter (PDF pp.4–18).** Scope and sequence (pp.4–5, identical tables to the Pupil's Book); Introduction — About Jordan Team Together, vocabulary approach (p.6); Course components (p.7); Unit walkthrough (pp.8–9); Course features incl. Picture dictionary and the two Review pages (pp.10–11); Icons (p.12); Assessment — monitoring progress and exam readiness, happy/sad face technique (p.13); How to work with phonics / with projects (pp.14–15); Games bank — flashcard and segmenting games (pp.16–17); Classroom language (p.18… greeting the class, game language).
- **Welcome — Hello! (PDF pp.19–21).** Mapping page, then notes: presentation showing Pupil's Book pages 4–5, naming Hala, Lama, Meg, Malek, Bill, Tom; Colours and numbers spread; extra TPR activity with Number flashcards.
- **Unit 1 — Family and friends (PDF pp.22–41).** Objectives: introduce yourself; say family members' names. Lessons 1–11 notes as per the pupil architecture (Vocabulary ×2, Language practice, Story Values, Language practice, Phonics Ss/Dd and Cc/Aa, Project: Finger puppets — materials cite Pupil's Book page 12), **plus Lesson 12 Review and Lesson 13 Review** (the latter keyed to the Activity Book).
- **Unit 2 — My school bag (PDF pp.42–61).** Objectives: talk about school objects; use have got for possession. Lessons 1–11 (Phonics Pp/Rr, Ee/Bb; Project: A school bag; song-based Lesson 8 in the Activity Book) plus Lessons 12–13 Review.
- **Unit 3 — Our classroom (PDF pp.62–81).** Objectives: talk about classroom things; say where things are. Lessons 1–11 (Phonics Tt/Mm, Ww/Ii; Project: A pen pot) plus Lessons 12–13 Review.
- **Unit 4 — My favourite toy (PDF pp.82–100).** Objectives: talk about favourite toys and their colours. Lessons 1–11 (Phonics CVC words ×2; Project: A toy box, PDF pp.97–98) plus Lesson 12 Review (PDF p.99) and Lesson 13 Review (PDF p.100).
- **Meg's Learning Club (PDF pp.101–104).** Mapping (listening: identifying simple words; segmenting simple spoken sentences); notes incl. the eyes-closed finger-tracking activity and the "My favourite day is Sunday" draw-and-write task (days of the week).
- **Extra resources (PDF p.105).** Pupil's Book Progress path (Pupil's Book pages 44–45) and Activity Book Fun time guidance.
- **Glossary (PDF p.106).** Word list with IPA, e.g. blue, pen, pencil, train, web.
- **Notes (PDF pp.107–111) and Image credits (PDF p.112).**

## Grammar and vocabulary spine for the book
- Mirrors the Semester 1 Pupil's Book exactly (numbers/colours; family; school bag + have got; classroom + place; toys + colour/feelings; phonics s–i + CVC) and adds the teacher layer: per-lesson language analysis, flashcard inventories (numbered flashcards cited by lesson, e.g. Flashcards 37–38, 50–53), answer keys inside the notes (the string "Answer key" appears 93 times), and photocopiable references (16 mentions).
- Method spine: presentation–practice lesson staging, TPR and games for this age band, phonics blending routine (isolated phonemes blended, e.g. the sad example on the How-to page), project cycle ending in acting out/sharing, and continuous assessment via observation techniques rather than printed tests.

## Honest negatives
- The guide does NOT contain: printed test papers, a separate audioscripts section (audio scripts are not reproduced; "Audioscripts" never appears), a wordlist section as such (only the one-page Glossary), or Arabic-language support for teachers.
- Edition mismatch in this set: this first-edition guide teaches Review lessons and a Progress path that the second-edition Pupil's Book held alongside it (g01-student-term-1-nccd.md) no longer presents in the same way — a teacher using both together would find the guide referencing material the revised pupil book dropped/renumbered.
- Answer keys are embedded in running notes rather than collected, so checking a full unit's answers means reading every lesson's notes.
- Copy defects: none material; facsimile pupil pages extract with the same decorative-header garbling as the pupil books.

Differences are metadata only: this copy was produced via Acrobat Distiller, is tagged, carries an AcroForm layer, has no Minhaji logo on the imprint page, and is larger on disk (56,355,316 bytes vs 41,848,999) because of its different image compression — not because of extra content. They are NOT two different books and NOT two editions or two scans of different printings.

## The book itself
- Verified title: *Jordan Team Together, Grade 1: Teacher's Book, Semester 2* (cover + indexing box, PDF p.2).
- Series/publisher: Jordan Team Together; National Center for Curriculum Development, Ministry of Education, Jordan. Author: **Nick Coates**; thanks to Sian Mavor and Wendy Cherry. © Pearson Education Limited and York Press Ltd. 2024.
- Edition: First edition (trial), 2024 / 1445. ISBN 978-9923-41-729-4. Deposit (2024/10/5990). Adoption: Supreme Council decision No. 9/2024; Board of Education decision No. 186/2024 (meeting No. 6/2024, 17/11/2024), for 2024/2025.
- Cover committee: "Evaluation and Adaptation Committee" (Al-Shara'h; Abuarman; Bani Abdelrahman; AlShboul; AlKaraki; Al-Omari).
- Copy facts: source file 56,355,316 bytes; 104 PDF pages. Printed = PDF − 2 (printed folios 100–101 visible at PDF pp.102–103).
- Text layer: full and identical in substance to the minhaji copy. Identity verified from rendered cover/imprint.

## Full unit/lesson map
PDF page numbers (printed = PDF − 2). Identical to the minhaji copy.
- **Front matter (PDF pp.4–18).** Scope and sequence (pp.4–5); Introduction (p.6); Course components (p.7); Unit walkthrough (pp.8–9); Course features/icons (pp.10–11); Assessment — monitoring progress and exam readiness (p.12); How to work with phonics / projects (pp.13–14); Games bank (pp.15–16); Classroom language (p.17); Notes (p.18).
- **Welcome — Who's this? (PDF pp.19–21).** Mapping; shake/clap action-song notes; classroom objects and toys revision.
- **Unit 5 — My body (PDF pp.22–39).** Objectives: name body parts; talk about what people have got. Story answer key (pictures showing Lama and Hala); Phonics Hh/Ll, Gg/Ff; Project: A funny friend (Pupil's Book page 12).
- **Unit 6 — My blue jacket (PDF pp.40–57).** Objectives: clothes; what you are wearing; where clothes are. Phonics Jj/Uu (Pupil's Book page 18), Oo/Nn; Project: Paper dolls (Pupil's Book page 20).
- **Unit 7 — They're tigers! (PDF pp.58–75).** Objectives: name animals. Hala/Fred exchange introducing May the snake in Lesson 7 notes; Phonics Zz/Kk/Qq, Xx/Vv/Yy; Project: Animal masks (Pupil's Book page 28).
- **Unit 8 — I like cakes (PDF pp.76–93).** Objectives: food; likes and dislikes. Happy/sad face technique in Story notes; Phonics CVC words ×2; Project: A food plate (Pupil's Book page 36).
- **Meg's Learning Club (PDF pp.94–96).** Road safety ("Cross here!", "Stop!"); Activity Book practice.
- **Extra resources (PDF p.97); Glossary with IPA (PDF p.98); Notes (PDF pp.99–103); Image credits (PDF p.104).**

## Grammar and vocabulary spine for the book
- Identical to the minhaji copy: teacher notes for the Semester 2 spine — body + has got, clothes + wearing/location, animals + "They're …", food + likes/dislikes; phonics h, l, g, f, j, u, o, n, z, k, q, x, v, y and CVC blending; embedded answer keys, games bank, classroom-language bank and assessment guidance in the front matter.

## Honest negatives
- This is a duplicate holding of g01-teacher-term-2.md's book; filing both as separate books would double-count the Semester 2 teacher guide.
- No tests, audioscripts or collected answer key inside the guide; no Arabic support; no Review lessons in this semester's guide.
- Copy defects: none; this is the cleaner production file (tagged, Distiller output), differing from its twin only in file plumbing and the absent Minhaji logo.

# Jordan — English Grade 2 Jordan Team Together, Grade 2: Pupil's Book, Semester 1 — FULLY READ + CITATION CHECK PASSED 2026-11-03 (anchors 20/20)

## The book itself
- Verified title (cover + imprint p.ii): *Jordan Team Together, Grade 2: Pupil's Book, Semester 1* — "Pupil's Book with Digital Resources". Series: Jordan Team Together (Jordan adaptation of Pearson's Team Together).
- Publisher: The National Center for Curriculum Development and Evaluation (NCCD), Amman, for the Hashemite Kingdom of Jordan Ministry of Education. © Pearson Education Limited and York Press Ltd.
- Authors of the original work: Susannah Reed and Lesley Koustaff, with Kay Bentley (thanked). Jordan Adaptation Committee: Prof. Fawwaz Mohammad Al-Abed Al-Haq (Head), Dr. Manal Fahed Aburumman (Coordinator), Prof. Sabri Shehadeh AlShboul, Dr. Zeina Mohammad AlKaraki, Dr. Adnan Ahmad Al-Omari.
- Edition: First edition (trial) 2025; **Second edition (revised and updated) 2026** — this copy. ISBN 978-9923-66-046-1. National Library deposit (2026/1/384). Adopted by Higher Council of NCCD meeting No. 3/2025 (27/3/2025) and Board of Education decision No. 30/2025 for the 2026/2027 academic year. Printed 2025, reprinted 2026.
- Copy facts: PDF 84 pages; 50,726,026 bytes. Printed folio = PDF page − 2 (printed p.4 = PDF p.6). Content runs printed pp.4–51 (PDF 6–52, incl. acknowledgements/image credits on printed p.50 area, PDF 52). PDF pp.53–84 are the cut-out appendix.
- Text-layer state: full text layer on all content pages. The cut-out appendix (PDF 53–84) is image-only picture cards (flashcards headed e.g. "Unit 1 Page 9", "Unit 4 Page 37 — My family"), rendered and visually read; final PDF p.84 is blank.
- Cast used throughout: Tom, Polly, Sami, Laila, Bo (robot), Atomic (cat); "Helpers Team" stories; "Ask Siraj!" culture pages.

## Full unit/lesson map
Every main unit follows the same 10-lesson shape: L1–2 Vocabulary & Grammar, L3–4 Story (Helpers Team), L5–6 Vocabulary & Grammar, L7 Skills, L8 Culture/Project, L9 English in action, L10 Phonics.

- **Welcome — Hello!** (printed pp.4–7): characters introduced; greetings and introductions. Vocabulary: numbers one–ten; colours black, blue, brown, green, orange, pink, purple, red, white, yellow. Grammar/functions: What's your name? My name's… / I'm…; How old are you? I'm…; How many…?; What colour is it? Value: Be friendly.
- **Unit 1 — Look at my toys!** (pp.8–15): Inside toys: action figure, building set, car, dinosaur, doll, plane, puzzle, robot, teddy, yoyo. Outside toys: ball, bike, kite, scooter, slide, swing. Grammar: What's this? It's a (car) / It's an (action figure); Is it a (swing)? Yes, it is. / No, it isn't; Can I play with (it), please? Story: a giant mystery object turns out to be a teddy robot. Culture/Project (L8): The Children's Museum in Amman — make a poster for the museum. English in action: Sharing your toys. Phonics: i-e, a-e (bike, five, cake, plane); tongue twister built on nine cakes / five bikes / a plane.
- **Unit 2 — Where's my pen?** (pp.16–23): Classroom objects: bag, book, chair, computer, crayon, cupboard, desk, eraser, pen, pencil, pencil case, pencil sharpener, ruler, scissors, shelf, table. Grammar: This is (my pencil case) / These are (my books); Where's (the pencil)? It's in/on/under (the desk); Can I borrow (it), please? Yes. Here you are. Story: Bo makes a mess; the class tidies up and Atomic the cat is found under the table (Value: Be tidy). Culture/Project: "My school" in Madaba (Jamal) — hall, library, playground, classroom, teacher; make a plan of your school. English in action: Borrowing things. Phonics: sh, ch (shelf, shop, chair, chick).
- **Unit 3 — Move your body!** (pp.24–31): Body parts: arms, face, feet, fingers, hands, head, knees, legs, toes, tummy. Movement: bend, clap, move, stamp, stretch, touch. Grammar: I've got (one face / ten toes); imperatives Move (your body)! Touch (your head)!; compliment What a cool (dinosaur)! Story: the team builds a robot, Gymbot, who leads exercises (Value: Do exercise). Culture/Project: favourite school days — Mother's Day at a school in Irbid (Zeina) and Teachers' Day (5th October); make a poster about your favourite day at school. English in action: Paying a compliment. Phonics: ee, ar (feet, knees, arms, park).
- **Unit 4 — Meet my family** (pp.32–39): Family: aunt, brother, cousin, dad, grandad, granny, me, mum, sister, uncle. Face: ears, eyes, mouth, nose; hair fair/brown/red/black. Grammar: Who's this? It's (my uncle); He's got / She's got (blue eyes); His name's… / Her name's… Story: Laila's family photo and a lost ball; her baby cousin is named Talal (Value: Love your family). Culture/Project: "Grandad's present" — Nada's Grandad Ramzi (grey hair, brown eyes, happy smile); make a class art gallery. English in action: Introducing family and friends. Phonics: th (voiced/unvoiced) — brother, this, three, mouth.
- **Atomic's Learning Club — Language booster 1** (pp.40–41): opinion adjectives fantastic, cool, great, noisy, funny, scary with puppet, monster, helicopter, robot, computer, book; favourite-book dialogue (Jack and the Beanstalk; Little Red Riding Hood; The Gingerbread Man, retold by Mandy Loader, "Pre-A1"); Is it (funny)? Yes, it is. / No, it isn't.
- **Atomic's Learning Club — Science (CLIL)** (pp.42–43): "What are our senses?" — five senses (sight, hearing, smell, taste, touch) matched to eyes, ears, nose, tongue, hands/feet; make a sense box.
- **Festivals** (pp.44–49): Independence Day in Jordan (fireworks, flag, parade, celebration, decorations, air show, night, May; make a model firework); Earth Day (pick up, bottles, cans, paper, recycle, rubbish, plant, tree; make an Earth Day handprint); Kite Festival Day (kite, string, bird, sky, fish, tail, horse, teddy; windy spring weather; make a kite).
- **Cut-outs** (PDF 53–84): picture cards keyed to unit pages.

## Grammar and vocabulary spine for the book
- Introductions: What's your name? / How old are you?; numbers 1–10; 10 colours.
- Naming and guessing objects: What's this? It's a/an…; Is it a…? Yes, it is. / No, it isn't.
- Singular/plural demonstratives: This is… / These are…; possession with my.
- Location: Where's…? It's in/on/under…
- Body and having: I've got + number + body part; imperatives for movement.
- People description: Who's this? It's my…; He's/She's got + face/hair; His/Her name's…
- Polite functions: Can I play with it, please?; Can I borrow it, please? — Yes. Here you are.; compliments (What a cool…!).
- Phonics spine: i-e/a-e → sh/ch → ee/ar → th/th.

## Honest negatives
- No ISBN barcode page beyond the imprint; no separate answer key (answers live in the Teacher's Book).
- The scope-and-sequence (PDF pp.4–5) is unnumbered front matter; printed folios only begin with the Welcome unit (printed p.4).
- Cut-out appendix pages carry almost no text by design (picture cards only) — nothing textual is missing there.
- Text extraction garbles some song/chant layout and drops the letter "f" in a few ligatured words; page images confirm the printed text is complete.

# Jordan — English Grade 2 Jordan Team Together, Grade 2: Activity Book, Semester 1 — FULLY READ + CITATION CHECK PASSED 2026-11-03 (anchors 18/18)

## The book itself
- Verified title (cover + imprint p.ii): *Jordan Team Together, Grade 2: Activity Book, Semester 1* — the workbook companion to the Grade 2 Pupil's Book Semester 1 (Jordan Team Together, Pearson/York Press, published by the National Center for Curriculum Development and Evaluation, Amman).
- Author of the original work: Jill Leighton, with Lesley Koustaff and Susannah Reed (thanked). Same Jordan Adaptation Committee as the Pupil's Book (Head: Prof. Fawwaz Mohammad Al-Abed Al-Haq; Coordinator: Dr. Manal Fahed Aburumman).
- Edition: First edition (trial) 2025; **Second edition (revised and updated) 2026** — this copy. ISBN 978-9923-66-048-5. National Library deposit (2026/1/387). Board of Education decision No. 31/2025; adopted for 2026/2027. Printed 2025, reprinted 2026.
- Copy facts: PDF 56 pages; 24,714,521 bytes. Printed folio = PDF page − 2 (contents printed p.1 = PDF p.3; last content printed p.51 = PDF p.53). PDF p.54 = image credits; PDF pp.55–56 are blank (no text layer, visually blank).
- Text-layer state: full text layer throughout the workbook pages and picture dictionary; only the two trailing blank pages have none.

## Full unit/lesson map
Contents (printed): Welcome Hello! p.2 · 1 Look at my toys! p.6 · 2 Where's my pen? p.14 · 3 Move your body! p.22 · 4 Meet my family p.30 · Language booster 1 p.38 · Festivals: Independence Day in Jordan p.40, Earth Day p.42, Kite Festival Day p.44 · Picture dictionary p.46.
Each unit mirrors the Pupil's Book lessons (Lessons 1–2 vocabulary/grammar, Lesson 4 story follow-up, Lessons 6–7 skills, Lesson 8 culture, Lessons 9–10 English in action + phonics) plus an Extra practice page per unit.

- **Welcome** (pp.2–5): trace character names (Polly, Sami, Tom, Bo, Laila); listen-and-number introductions; complete name/age dialogues; trace-and-match numbers; read/trace/colour colour words with What colour is it? — It's (blue).
- **Unit 1 Toys** (pp.6–13): PB p.8 picture questions (balloon counts, teddy/car colours); trace-and-match the 10 inside toys + robot; listening tick; circle It's a robot/dinosaur-type items; story recall (order the teddy-robot story lines; Values tick); outside toys find-and-colour with Is it a ball? Yes, it is. / No, it isn't.; skills writing (What's this? It's a… It's (red).); culture match (museum, puppet, climbing wall); phonics i-e/a-e word-picture matching (bike, cake, gate, snake, kite, plane) and the "Sharing your toys" dialogue completion; Extra practice word/picture work.
- **Unit 2 Classroom** (pp.14–21): PB p.16 questions; trace-and-match classroom words; This is/These are read-match-and-colour; story recall and Values; prepositions in/on/under circle-the-answer exercises; skills read-and-tick with model sentences (pencil case on the desk, pencil in the pencil case…); culture Yes/No (hall, library, classroom, playground); "Borrowing things" dialogue completion; phonics sh/ch (shelf, chair, shirt, shell, cheese, chain, shop, chick); Extra practice preposition writing.
- **Unit 3 Body** (pp.22–29): PB p.24 questions; trace-and-match body words; listen-and-number; robot description writing (I've got a blue head and a … face…); story recall/Values; movement verbs (touch, stretch, move, clap, stamp, bend) matching and ordering; skills true/false on body counts (I've got ten fingers ✔ …); culture Yes/No on the Teachers' Day text (incl. the false statement that Teachers' Day is in May); phonics ee/ar completion; "Paying a compliment" dialogue; Extra practice monster-body reading (e.g. six arms, three knees descriptions).
- **Unit 4 Family** (pp.30–37): PB p.32 questions; family word writing/matching; listen-and-number; story completion (baby cousin Talal; mum and dad); wordsearch (eyes, ears, hair, nose, mouth); description circling (She's got a big/small mouth…); skills: read-and-tick descriptions and write about a family member (His/Her name's…; He's/She's got … hair and … eyes); culture match/circle on "Grandad's present" (Nada, grey hair, happy smile, picture); phonics th completion (brother, mouth, three, this); "Introducing family and friends" dialogue; Extra practice: identify family members from written descriptions.
- **Language booster 1** (pp.38–39): puppet, book, helicopter, computer, monster, robot with Is it funny/noisy? Yes, it is. / No, it isn't.; draw-and-write toy descriptions with fantastic/cool/great/funny/scary; order and complete the favourite-book dialogue.
- **Festivals** (pp.40–45): Independence Day (match/trace/colour: parade, night, decoration, May, celebration, air show, flag, fireworks; read-and-match sentences; draw a firework); Earth Day (trace-and-match paper, cans, tree, bottles, rubbish, plant, pick up, recycle; count-and-write; "I recycle… / I plant a tree"); Kite Festival Day (look-and-write string, kite, horse, teddy, sky, bird, fish, tail; colour-by-number; complete windy/spring/kite/tail/teddy sentences).
- **Picture dictionary** (pp.46–51): W Numbers and colours; 1 Inside and outside toys; 2 Classroom objects; 3 Body parts and body movement; 4 Family and face; F Festivals word bank — picture-labelled word lists for the whole semester.

## Grammar and vocabulary spine for the book
- Writing/tracing practice for every Pupil's Book set: numbers and colours; toys; classroom objects + in/on/under; body parts + I've got; family + He's/She's got descriptions.
- Sentence-level work stays at copying, circling, matching and gap-filling model sentences; short guided writing only in Skills pages (describe a toy, a robot, a family member, your kite/firework).
- Phonics practice mirrors the Pupil's Book: i-e/a-e, sh/ch, ee/ar, th.

## Honest negatives
- No answer key in the Activity Book itself (keys are in the Teacher's Book).
- Two final PDF pages are completely blank; the credits page (PDF 54) is the last printed content.
- Workbook audio tracks (e.g. 1.5, 2.5, 1.18) are referenced but, as in the Pupil's Book, the audio itself is a separate digital resource, not in the PDF.

# Jordan — English Grade 2 Jordan Team Together, Grade 2: Teacher's Book, Semester 1 — FULLY READ + CITATION CHECK PASSED 2026-11-03 (anchors 18/18)

## The book itself
- Verified title (cover + imprint p.ii): *Jordan Team Together, Grade 2: Teacher's Book, Semester 1* — "Teacher's Book with Digital Resources". Series: Jordan Team Together (Pearson Education Limited and York Press Ltd), published by the National Center for Curriculum Development, Amman.
- Author: Catherine Zgouras, with Magdalena Custodio and Victoria Bewick (thanked). Evaluation and Adaptation Committee as in the Pupil's Book (Head: Prof. Fawwaz Mohammad Al-Abed Al-Haq).
- Edition: **First edition (trial) 2025** — this copy. ISBN 978-9923-41-928-1. National Library deposit (2025/3/1673). Board of Education decision No. 32/2025; adopted for the **2025/2026** academic year. Note the mismatch: this first-edition Teacher's Book predates the Pupil's Book's second edition (2026, for 2026/2027) held in the same set.
- Copy facts: PDF 128 pages; 38,738,672 bytes. Printed folio = PDF page − 2 (contents printed p.1 = PDF p.3; lesson notes printed pp.24–123; glossary printed pp.124–125 = PDF pp.126–127; image credits PDF p.128). Full text layer on every page.

## What the guide adds (scripts, keys, method)
- **Front matter (printed pp.2–23):** full Scope and sequence (same tables as the Pupil's Book); Introduction — the course is a 7-level primary course taking pupils from Pre-A1 to B1 (CEFR), with rationale for pupils and teachers; Course components and the 10-lesson unit division; a Unit walkthrough using annotated Pupil's Book/Activity Book pages; an **Assessment** section covering English Benchmark, mini-whiteboards, independent learning and long-term formative assessment, and analytic rubrics; **How to…** guides (work with projects, mixed-ability groups, stories, posters); **Classroom language** phrase lists (e.g. pair/group work: "Find a partner. Get into twos/threes."); a **Games bank** (e.g. Which card have you got?, Countdown, games with songs and chants, Answer and draw points).
- **Lesson notes (printed pp.24–123):** for every Pupil's Book and Activity Book lesson — lesson aims and target language, materials, "Starting the lesson" and step-by-step procedures, **full audioscripts** (e.g. Welcome track W1: a presenter introduces Tom, who introduces himself), **answer keys** for Pupil's Book and Activity Book activities, Assessment for Learning boxes (setting aims, monitoring, peer learning, independent learning techniques such as Lollipop sticks and Traffic lights), fast-finisher/extra activities, and per-lesson **Global Scale of English (GSE)** can-do descriptors keyed to skills.
- **Mapping pages** before each unit tie the unit to Jordan curriculum themes (e.g. Unit 1: Theme 1 Recreation, Theme 2 Culture) and the Scope and Sequence Matrix.
- **Glossary (printed pp.124–125):** every taught word with IPA transcription, organised by unit (Welcome, Units 1–4, Learning Club, Festivals).
- Reproduced Pupil's Book and Activity Book pages are interleaved with the notes, so the guide is self-contained.

## Full unit/lesson map (lesson notes)
- Welcome — Hello!: notes pp.24–32 (characters, numbers, colours; audioscripts W1–W14; GSE mapping).
- Unit 1 — Look at my toys!: unit objectives p.33 ("to name toys; to ask and answer about toys"); notes pp.33–50.
- Unit 2 — Where's my pen?: unit objectives p.51; notes pp.51–68.
- Unit 3 — Move your body!: unit objectives p.69; notes pp.69–86.
- Unit 4 — Meet my family: unit objectives p.87; notes pp.87–103 (phonics th notes p.103).
- Language booster 1: pp.104–107. Science CLIL "What are our senses?": pp.108–111.
- Festivals: Independence Day in Jordan pp.112–115; Earth Day pp.116–119; Kite Festival Day pp.120–123 (ends with a Diversity/Support page).
- Glossary pp.124–125; image credits final page.
- Content taught is exactly the Pupil's Book Semester 1 spine: toys + It's a/Is it a…?; classroom objects + in/on/under; body + I've got; family + He's/She's got; phonics i-e/a-e, sh/ch, ee/ar, th.

## Grammar and vocabulary spine for the book
Identical language spine to the Pupil's Book Semester 1 (see g02-student-term-1.md); the Teacher's Book adds the pedagogical spine: present → practise → produce with songs/chants, story acting, projects, and formative assessment in every lesson, all referenced to GSE/CEFR Pre-A1 descriptors and to the Jordan curriculum theme matrix.

## Honest negatives
- Edition mismatch inside the set: Teacher's Book is the 2025 first (trial) edition for 2025/2026, while the Pupil's Book and Activity Book in hand are the revised second edition for 2026/2027; page references were consistent in everything sampled, but the guide was not re-issued with the pupil books.
- Tests themselves (unit tests and the End-of-semester test) are only described/referenced — they are separate digital resources, not printed in this book.
- Audioscripts are printed in full, but the audio files are digital resources outside the PDF.

# Jordan — English Grade 2 Jordan Team Together, Grade 2: Pupil's Book, Semester 2 — FULLY READ + CITATION CHECK PASSED 2026-11-03 (anchors 20/20)

## The book itself
- Verified title (cover + imprint p.ii): *Jordan Team Together, Grade 2: Pupil's Book, Semester 2* — "Pupil's Book with Digital Resources". Series: Jordan Team Together (Pearson Education Limited and York Press Ltd), published by the National Center for Curriculum Development, Amman.
- Authors: Susannah Reed and Lesley Koustaff, with Kay Bentley (thanked). Evaluation and Adaptation Committee: Prof. Fawwaz Mohammad Al-Abed Al-Haq (Head), Dr. Manal Fahed Aburumman (Coordinator), Prof. Sabri Shehadeh AlShboul, Dr. Zeina Mohammad AlKaraki, Dr. Adnan Ahmad Al-Omari.
- Edition: **First edition (trial) 2025** — this copy. ISBN 978-9923-863-23-7. National Library deposit (2025/10/6109). Adopted by Higher Council meeting No. 8/2025 (16/10/2025) and Board of Education decision No. 207/2025 for the **2025/2026** academic year.
- Copy facts: PDF 80 pages; 48,099,392 bytes. Printed folio = PDF page − 2 (printed p.4 = PDF p.6); content printed pp.4–49 (PDF 6–51), acknowledgements/credits PDF p.52, cut-outs PDF pp.53–80.
- Text-layer state: text layer on all content pages; cut-out pages (PDF 53–80) carry only their short headers (picture cards). Every page carries the red Arabic watermark "نسخة قيد الإعداد والتجهيز" ("copy under preparation") — visible diagonally on the cover render too. This is a preparation/trial copy, unlike the finalised Semester 1 second edition.

## Full unit/lesson map
Same 10-lesson unit architecture as Semester 1. Contents: Welcome Hello! p.4 · 5 Help the animals! p.8 · 6 Do you like peas? p.16 · 7 It's windy! p.24 · 8 Who's at home? p.32 · Language booster 2 p.40 · Natural Science: What's a beach like? p.42 · Festivals: World Braille Day p.44, World Environment Day p.46, Hijri New Year p.48 · Cutouts p.51.

- **Welcome — Hello!** (pp.4–7): revision of Semester 1 — toys, body parts, family, classroom objects and the Semester 1 grammar (What's this? It's a…; Where's the pencil? It's in/on/under…; I've got…; Who's this? He's/She's got…), through a "Be tidy!" story (a lost doll with red hair and blue eyes) and the toy-sharing dialogue.
- **Unit 5 — Help the animals!** (pp.8–15): Animals: cat, duck, fish, hamster, mouse, parrot, rabbit, snake, spider, tortoise. Animal body: feathers, fur, shell, tail, teeth, wings. Adjectives: big, long, short, small. Grammar: What are these? They're (cats); It's got (big teeth); irregular plurals one mouse – two mice, one fish – two fish. Story: mysterious eggs hatch into baby rabbits and a baby tortoise (Value: Take care of animals). Culture/Project: "Animals in Jordan" — the oryx, the national animal of Jordan (white and brown, two horns, lives in the desert), and the striped hyena; organise a class rescue centre. English in action: Asking about animals (Have you got a toy animal?). Phonics: ai (rain, tail) and double letters bb, ll, rr (rabbit, parrot, shell).
- **Unit 6 — Do you like peas?** (pp.16–23): Food (1): apple, banana, bean, carrot, orange, pasta, pea, potato, rice, tomato. Food (2): bread, cheese, chicken, eggs, fish, juice, meat, milk, water. Grammar: I like (apples) / I don't like (pasta); Do you like (cheese)? Yes, I do. / No, I don't; joining with and/too, but, or; plurals tomato–tomatoes, potato–potatoes. Story: planting a vegetable garden; "Is rice a plant? Is pasta a plant?" (Value: Eat healthy food). Culture/Project: "Bread" — Jordanian breads: taboon (eaten with hummus), shrak (eaten with mansaf), manakish (with za'atar and olive oil), labaneh; make a favourite-meal poster. English in action: Asking for food and drink (Can I have a carrot, please? / Can I have some juice, please?). Phonics: ea (beak, peas, head, feather).
- **Unit 7 — It's windy!** (pp.24–31): Weather: cloudy, cold, foggy, hot, rainy, snowy, stormy, sunny, windy. Clothes: boots, coat, jumper, T-shirt, shoes, shorts, skirt, trousers. Grammar: What's the weather like? It's (cloudy); I'm wearing (a T-shirt and shorts). Story: wild weather at the beach — fog, wind, sun — and Atomic found in a sandcastle (Value: Be prepared). Culture/Project: Eid al-Fitr described by Saleem (breakfast, mosque, mamoul biscuits, presents, new clothes from the souq/market; date changes each year because of the moon); make a festival guide. English in action: Giving instructions (Put on your coat! / Take off your jumper!). Phonics: ir, or (bird, shirt, fork, shorts).
- **Unit 8 — Who's at home?** (pp.32–39): Home: balcony, bathroom, bedroom, flat, garage, garden, hall, house, kitchen, living room. Chores: clean the floor, lay the table, make the bed, tidy up, wash the car, water the plants. Grammar: Where's my dad? He's in the (garden) / He isn't in the (bedroom); present continuous for chores: He's/She's (tidying up). Story: a map leads to a "treasure" that turns out to be a time capsule under the swing (Value: Be curious). Culture/Project: "Unusual homes" — a treehouse, a dining room on a boat, a home in a cave in Petra, a tent bedroom in Wadi Rum, a house in Dana; make a class book about unusual homes. English in action: Asking for and offering to help (Can you help me, please? Sure. What can I do?). Phonics: oo (moon, bedroom, look, book).
- **Language booster 2** (pp.40–41): sport/activity verbs — play football, play basketball, play tennis, play table tennis, play badminton, play hockey, ride a bike, fly a kite — with I enjoy… / I don't enjoy… and suggestions (Let's play… / What about…?).
- **Natural Science (CLIL)** (pp.42–43): "What's a beach like?" — sea, sand, shells, rock pools, crabs, starfish; note the text also models was/wasn't/were/weren't in a short beach-day frame ("It was a sunny day. We were at the beach. It wasn't cold.").
- **Festivals** (pp.44–49): World Braille Day (read, write, book, sign, touch, play, dots, game; project: write your name in Braille); World Environment Day (protect, nature, ocean, animals, share, think, talk, world; make an ocean picture); Hijri New Year (family, meal, gift, neighbours, prayer, peace, moon, kindness; make a kindness card).

## Grammar and vocabulary spine for the book
- Plurals and describing animals: They're…; It's got + body part/adjective; big/small/long/short.
- Likes: I like / I don't like; Do you like…? Yes, I do. / No, I don't; and/too/but/or joining; requests Can I have (a/some)…, please?
- Weather and clothing: What's the weather like? It's…; I'm wearing…; imperatives Put on… / Take off…
- Home and present continuous: He's/She's in the (room); He's/She's + verb-ing for six household chores; offering help.
- Phonics spine: ai + doubled consonants → ea → ir/or → oo.

## Honest negatives
- This Semester 2 copy is a first-edition trial/preparation copy: the "under preparation" Arabic watermark is printed on every page including the cover, and the publisher is named "The National Center for Curriculum Development" (without "and Evaluation") — it is not the finalised second-edition standard of the Semester 1 books.
- Two extraction-level folio errors appear in the PDF text layer ("ninety" on the story page printed 34; "seventy-four" on printed p.40) — the printed folios themselves are correct; these are text-layer artefacts.
- No answer key in the Pupil's Book; the beach CLIL's was/were model sentences are presented for reading only, with no grammar teaching around them.

## Full unit/lesson map
Contents (printed): Welcome Hello! p.2 · 5 Help the animals! p.6 · 6 Do you like peas? p.14 · 7 It's windy! p.22 · 8 Who's at home? p.30 · Language booster 2 p.38 · Festivals: World Braille Day p.40, World Environment Day p.42, Hijri New Year p.44 · Picture Dictionary p.46.
Structure mirrors Semester 1's Activity Book: per unit — vocabulary/grammar lessons keyed to Pupil's Book pages, story recall (Lesson 4), skills (Lesson 7), culture (Lesson 8), English in action + phonics (Lessons 9–10), Extra practice page.

- **Welcome** (pp.2–5): match-and-say toys; complete the toy-sharing dialogue; read-and-draw a robot from a written description (I've got four eyes and three ears…); family description ticking (fair hair, big brown eyes, small nose); classroom preposition writing (in/on/under + desk, pencil case, chair, shelf).
- **Unit 5 Animals** (pp.6–13): PB p.8 questions (scooter colour, bikes, frogs); write-and-match the 10 animals; They're (fish/cats/mice/spiders) writing; story recall (white mice, ducks, eggs); animal-body descriptions (It's got short fur / a long tail / a big shell / feathers and two wings / big teeth — Is it a…?); skills read-and-match descriptions; culture Yes/No on the oryx and hyena (incl. "It's the national animal of Jordan"); phonics ai/bb/ll/rr word-building (parrot, rabbit, tortoise, shell…) and the "Asking about animals" dialogue; Extra practice counting and long description matching.
- **Unit 6 Food** (pp.14–21): PB p.16 questions; write-and-match Food (1); make like/don't like sentences true for you; story completion (planting vegetables; pasta/peas/potatoes); Do you like…? Yes, I do. / No, I don't. writing with Food (2) words built from letter chunks (cheese, eggs, milk, juice…); skills read-and-tick dialogues; culture circle-the-answer on Jordanian breads (mansaf with shrak; taboon with hummus; manakish with za'atar and olive oil); phonics ea and the "Asking for food and drink" dialogue; Extra practice wordsearch and sentence ordering (Do you like fish?).
- **Unit 7 Weather** (pp.22–29): PB p.24 questions; write the 9 weather words; It's… / It isn't… weather writing; story true/false (wild weather); I'm wearing… sentence building with the 8 clothes words; skills: write about the weather and clothes for Amman (hot and sunny) and Ajloun (cold and snowy); culture/festival-guide follow-up; phonics ir/or word completion (shorts, skirt, torch, corn, girl) and the "Giving instructions" dialogue (Put on your…! / Take off your…!); Extra practice clothes wordsearch (start/finish).
- **Unit 8 Home** (pp.30–37): PB p.32 questions (who's wearing an orange T-shirt; car colour; blue chair's room); write-and-match the 10 home words; He's/She's in/isn't in… writing; story circle-and-solve-the-mystery (the "treasure" is a time capsule); chores: complete the phrases (make the bed, lay the table, water the plants, clean the floor, wash the car, tidy up) and order present-continuous sentences (He's making the bed.); skills read-and-match (Where's Samia? — she's on the balcony, watering the plants, etc.); culture Yes/No on unusual homes (tent, boat, treehouse, cave, dining room); phonics oo word-building (bedroom, book, moon…) and the "Asking for help" dialogue; Extra practice room/chore writing.
- **Language booster 2** (pp.38–39): board-game ordering of the 8 activities (fly a kite, play hockey, play table tennis, play tennis, play badminton, ride a bike, play basketball, play football); I enjoy / I don't enjoy writing; order the suggestions dialogue; read-and-tick about Rashed (enjoys table tennis, badminton, tennis, football; doesn't enjoy basketball or hockey).
- **Festivals** (pp.40–45): World Braille Day (look-and-write read, sign, touch, play, dots, game, write, book; write the words game/book in Braille dots; read-and-match); World Environment Day (nature, ocean, animal, protect, share, talk, world, think; complete "We protect nature. Our world is special…"); Hijri New Year (gift, neighbours, kindness, meal, family, peace, moon, prayer; crossword; sentence completion).
- **Picture dictionary** (pp.46–51): W revision word-search page (toys/body/family/classroom mix); 5 Animals and animal body; 6 Food; 7 Weather and clothes; 8 Home and chores; F Festivals word bank.

## Grammar and vocabulary spine for the book
- Written practice for the Pupil's Book spine: They're… / It's got…; I like / I don't like / Do you like…?; It's (weather) / I'm wearing…; He's/She's in… + present-continuous chores.
- Exercise types: trace/write/match, circle, order-the-sentence, complete-the-dialogue, true/false, guided personal writing.
- Phonics practice mirrors the Pupil's Book: ai/bb/ll/rr, ea, ir/or, oo.

## Honest negatives
- No answer key in the Activity Book (keys in the Teacher's Book).
- Trial-copy status: "under preparation" watermark throughout; not a finalised edition.
- A code-mystery puzzle on the Unit 8 story page (printed p.32) extracts as symbol garbage in the text layer; it is a picture/symbol code activity, readable only from the page image.

# Jordan — English Grade 3 Jordan Team Together, Grade 3: Pupil's Book, Semester 1 — FULLY READ + CITATION CHECK PASSED 2026-11-03 (anchors 20/20)

## The book itself
- Verified title (cover + imprint p.ii): *Jordan Team Together, Grade 3: Pupil's Book, Semester 1* — "Pupil's Book with Digital Resources". Series: Jordan Team Together (Pearson Education Limited and York Press Ltd), published by the National Center for Curriculum Development and Evaluation, Amman.
- Author of the original work: **Lesley Koustaff** (single named author at Grade 3), with Kay Bentley and Sarah O'Connell (thanked). Jordan Adaptation Committee: Prof. Nayel Darweesh Al-Shara'h (Head), Dr. Manal Fahed Aburumman (Coordinator), Prof. Dina AbdulHameed Al-Jamal, Dr. Hamzah Ali Omari, Dr. Zeina Mohammad AlKaraki.
- Edition: First edition (trial) 2024; **Second edition (revised and updated) 2025** — this copy. ISBN 978-9923-41-911-3. National Library deposit (2025/3/1585). Board of Education decision No. 41/2024; adopted for 2025/2026. Printed 2024, reprinted 2025–2026.
- Copy facts: PDF 80 pages; 67,968,867 bytes. Printed folio = PDF page − 2 (printed p.4 = PDF p.6); content printed pp.4–47 (PDF 6–49), credits PDF p.50, cut-outs PDF pp.51–80.
- Text-layer state: full text layer on content pages; the cut-out appendix (PDF 51–80) is image-only picture cards headed by unit/page (e.g. "Unit 1 Page 9"), rendered and visually checked.

## Full unit/lesson map
Same 10-lesson unit architecture as Grade 2. Contents: Welcome Back to school p.4 · 1 It's a happy day! p.8 · 2 Let's dress up! p.16 · 3 The activity centre p.24 · 4 I want to be a teacher! p.32 · Language booster 1 p.40 · Maths: How do we tell the time? p.42 · Festivals: Months and seasons p.44, Independence Day p.46 · Cut-outs p.49.

- **Welcome — Back to school** (pp.4–7): alphabet (letter names; spelling names — "How do you spell Asma? A-s-m-a"); numbers 11–20; demonstratives This is a pen / These are pens / That is a pen / Those are pens (Polly's bag holds fourteen pencils and twenty crayons). English in action: Asking for and spelling names.
- **Unit 1 — It's a happy day!** (pp.8–15): Daily routines: get up, have breakfast, go to school, have art lessons, play with my friends, go home, have lunch, do homework, have dinner, go to bed. Times of the day: morning, afternoon, evening, night. Grammar: I play / don't play; We have / don't have (lunch); When do you (go home)? We go home in the (afternoon). Story: an art lesson where blue and yellow make green and Bo ends up painted (Value: Work together). Culture/Project: "Traditional breakfasts" — Jordanian foul, falafel, hummus, tea, flatbread and mfarrakeh (potatoes and eggs); Japan (fish, pickles, rice, soup); UK (toast, cereal, tea); South Africa (corn porridge); make a breakfast menu. English in action: Saying the time (o'clock). Phonics: pl, bl (play, plane, plum, plug; blue, blond, black, block).
- **Unit 2 — Let's dress up!** (pp.16–23): Clothes: dress, sandals, socks, hat, scarf, jeans, pyjamas, trainers, baseball cap, shirt. Personal possessions: glasses, keys, handbag, backpack, phone, computer. Grammar: You're/They're wearing (jeans and trainers); possessives Our glasses are blue / Your keys are grey / Their phones are green. Story: dress-up day at school with teacher Miss Halima (Value: Recycle and reuse). Culture/Project: "Big parades" — Eid Al-Fitr dress (thobe, kuffiyah, ogal), kimonos in Japan, kilts in Scotland, floats and masks; make a special-day poster. English in action: Asking the price (How much is this baseball cap? — prices in JD). Phonics: dr, tr, br (dress, drum, drive, drop; trainers, tree, train, trip; bread, brush, brain).
- **Unit 3 — The activity centre** (pp.24–31): Activities: watch TV, play table tennis, read books, play badminton, play video games, play tennis, play basketball, roller skate, skateboard, do karate. Days of the week Sunday–Saturday + the weekend. Grammar: third person — He/She plays (video games); He/She doesn't watch TV; Does he do karate on Saturday? Yes, he does. / No, he doesn't. Story: "Where's Laila?" — her timetable of tennis, skateboarding and roller skating ends at a picnic (Value: Enjoy time with family and friends). Culture/Project: "Rugby fun" (Britain; fifteen players; kicking over the goal post scores three points) and Amer's tennis; make a book about favourite sports. English in action: Talking about favourite activities. Phonics: sk, sw, st (skateboard, skirt, skate, skip; swim, swing, sweet; star, stop, step, stand).
- **Unit 4 — I want to be a teacher!** (pp.32–39): Jobs: teacher, doctor, nurse, firefighter, astronaut, pilot, police officer, builder, chef, artist. Transport: bus, boat, underground, train, motorbike, car. Grammar: I want to be a (pilot); He/She goes to work by (bus). Story: a job fair — Aunt Maha, Mr Hani the pilot, Mrs Halima the chef, Mr Kareem the doctor; Aunt Maha goes home by plane (Value: Follow your dreams). Culture/Project: "The yellow school bus" in India; make a graph about how you go to school. English in action: Buying tickets (Four bus tickets, please. What time is the next bus?). Phonics: wh (what, when, wheel, whistle, whale).
- **Language booster 1** (pp.40–41): after-school activities and hobbies (watch TV, do homework, go to bed, go to sleep, read a book, play video games; drawing pictures, taking photos) with and/but joining (I watch TV and I play video games. I watch TV, but I don't read a book!).
- **Maths (CLIL)** (pp.42–43): "How do we tell the time?" — minute hand/hour hand; o'clock, quarter past, half past, quarter to, and five/ten/twenty/twenty-five past and to; make a clock.
- **Festivals** (pp.44–47): Months and seasons (the four seasons; spring = March, April, May; four-seasons tree project); Independence Day (flag, mansaf, fireworks, air show, crafts, band, parade, dessert; make a firework picture). Note: the festival tracks here are numbered F1 (Months and seasons) and F3 (Independence Day) — see Honest negatives.

## Grammar and vocabulary spine for the book
- Revision/extension of Grade 2: demonstratives this/these/that/those; alphabet and numbers to 20.
- Present simple (I/We) for routines with don't; When do you…? + in the morning/afternoon/evening, at night.
- Present continuous for wearing, with possessive adjectives our/your/their.
- Present simple third person (plays/doesn't; Does he/she…? Yes, he does. / No, he doesn't) with days of the week.
- Want to be + jobs; goes to work by + transport.
- Time: o'clock in the units, extended to quarter/half past/to in the Maths CLIL.
- Phonics spine shifts from vowel digraphs (Grade 2) to consonant blends: pl/bl → dr/tr/br → sk/sw/st → wh.

## Honest negatives
- Festival numbering skips F2: the Pupil's Book contains only F1 (Months and seasons) and F3 (Independence Day). The missing F2 is the original edition's Calgary Stampede unit, removed in the Jordan adaptation (its notes survive in the Teacher's Book — see g03-teacher-term-1.md).
- The scope-and-sequence writing column contains a typo ("caridnal numbers") in the source.
- No answer key in the Pupil's Book; cut-out pages are picture-only by design.

## Full unit/lesson map
Contents (printed): Welcome Back to school p.2 · 1 It's a happy day! p.6 · 2 Let's dress up! p.14 · 3 The activity centre p.22 · 4 I want to be a teacher! p.30 · Language booster p.38 · Festivals: Months and seasons p.40, Independence Day p.41 · Picture dictionary p.42.
Per unit: vocabulary/grammar lessons keyed to the Pupil's Book, story follow-up (Lesson 4), skills writing (Lesson 7), culture (Lesson 8), English in action + phonics (Lessons 9–10), Extra practice page.

- **Unit 2 Clothes** (pp.14–21): PB p.16 questions; clothes listening/circling; We're/You're/They're wearing sentence completion; story questions (dress-up day at 3 o'clock; Laila's blue dress); possessives crossword and our/your/their matching (backpack, phone, keys, computer, handbag, glasses); skills: "It's Tea Time!" reading (a garden tea gathering) with Yes/No and a letter to Grandma about dress-up day; culture parades (kilts, Japan, kimonos, floats, fancy shirts); phonics dr/tr/br word completion; "Asking the price" dialogue (12 JD / 20 JD).
- **Unit 3 Activities** (pp.22–29): PB p.24 questions; activities circling; He/She plays/doesn't matching and writing; story circling (Laila's timetable — she skateboards at 10 o'clock, doesn't roller skate at 9); Does she/he…? Yes, she does. / No, he doesn't. with days of the week; skills: read "Laith and Maher's week" and write a letter to Uncle Abbas; culture rugby correction exercises (a team has fifteen players; kick the ball over the goal post); phonics sk/sw/st; "Talking about favourite activities" dialogue matching.
- **Unit 4 Jobs** (pp.30–37): PB p.32 questions; jobs listen-and-match (chef, teacher, police officer, nurse, astronaut, builder, firefighter, doctor, artist, pilot); I want to be a/an… circling and writing; story circling (Mr Hani is a pilot; Maha goes home by plane; Bo and Atomic want to be artists); transport: He/She goes to work by train/car/boat/motorbike/underground writing; skills: "My family is cool!" reading (grandpa the doctor goes by car; aunt the artist by bus; brother the chef by motorbike) and a letter to Kareem about your future job; culture true/false on Indian school buses (they are yellow, not green); phonics wh and the "Buying tickets" intonation exercise.
- **Language booster** (pp.38–39): after-school activities; write and/but about Fadi; order the hobbies dialogue; read-and-circle Amina's hobbies (skateboarding, tennis, video games, reading).
- **Festivals** (pp.40–41): Months and seasons (month ordering; word snake winter/spring/autumn/summer; write about yourself — model: Jamal likes autumn, favourite month October); Independence Day (look-write-read: air show, dessert, flag, fireworks, band, mansaf, parades; a short completion text — Independence Day is celebrated in Jordan every year… children wave flags, watch an air show and fireworks, eat mansaf for dinner).
- **Picture dictionary** (pp.42–47): W Numbers (11–20); 1 Daily routines and times of the day; 2 Clothes and personal possessions; 3 Activities and days of the week; 4 Jobs and transport.

## Grammar and vocabulary spine for the book
- Written consolidation of the Pupil's Book spine: demonstratives; present simple I/We with don't; When do you…?; We're/You're/They're wearing + our/your/their; third-person plays/doesn't and Does he/she…?; want to be; goes to work by….
- Skills pages introduce short guided letters ("my day", a letter to Grandma, a letter to Uncle Abbas, a letter to Kareem) — the first extended writing in the series so far.
- Phonics mirrors the Pupil's Book blends pl/bl, dr/tr/br, sk/sw/st, wh.

## Honest negatives
- No answer key in the Activity Book (keys in the Teacher's Book).
- The PDF's final page is blank and PDF p.49 is a near-empty picture-dictionary divider; nothing else is missing.
- Prices in the shopping dialogue are in Jordanian dinars (JD), consistent with the Pupil's Book.
